**Employment and labour-insurance provision inventory, 26 September 2026.** These
source-backed branches are also absent from JP settings and production output. They are
separate from health/pension grade premiums and from insurer-paid unemployment benefits.

| Obligation                                    | Dated source-backed mechanism                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | Required input, output and independent closure case                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | Implemented?              | If not, why?                                                                              |
| --------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| JP-EI01 — insured-worker enrolment            | [MHLW enrolment guidance](https://www.mhlw.go.jp/stf/seisakunitsuite/bunya/0000147331.html) requires both an expected continuous employment of **at least 31 days** and contracted work of **at least 20 hours/week** for the ordinary insured-worker branch. The employer files the acquisition notice by the tenth of the month after insured status starts; later renewability can make an initially shorter contract covered from that later decision. The [enacted 2024 amendment explained by MHLW](https://www.mhlw.go.jp/content/11601000/001266437.pdf) lowers the weekly-hours threshold to **10 hours from 1 October 2028**. Student, day-worker, seasonal, officer and multi-employer exceptions need their separate statutory dispositions.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | Capture contracted weekly hours, expected duration and changes, actual insured dates, worker category and acquisition proof. Probe 19.99/20 hours and 30/31 expected days in December 2025, a later contract renewal, an older worker, and 9.99/10/19.99/20 hours on both sides of 1 October 2028. No premium may be treated as proof of filed insured status.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | No [IN-SYSTEM 2026-09-29] | Source exceptions and required inputs remain open; no JP sealed rule or production probe. |
| JP-EI02 — per-payment premium and rounding    | [MHLW's 2025](https://www.mhlw.go.jp/content/001401966.pdf) and [2026](https://www.mhlw.go.jp/content/001692566.pdf) tables set worker/employer rates per 1,000: **general 5.5/9 → 5/8.5; agriculture/forestry/fishery and sake 6.5/10 → 6/9.5; construction 6.5/11 → 6/10.5**, effective 1 April 2026 for the latter values. The tables name sector exceptions that instead use the general rate. [MHLW's collection guidance](https://www.mhlw.go.jp/www2/topics/seido/daijin/hoken/980916_3.htm) assesses the worker part on wages at each payment, including bonuses; its wage-withheld rounding drops a **fraction of exactly JPY0.50**, while direct cash collection rounds that fraction up, subject to an established different labour-management practice. [Kanagawa Labour Bureau](https://jsite.mhlw.go.jp/kanagawa-roudoukyoku/hourei_seido_tetsuzuki/roudou_hoken/hourei_seido/2024_00001.html) includes commuting and other taxable/non-taxable labour remuneration in the gross wage base before tax and social deductions. | Declare insured status, actual industry/subindustry, wage components and in-kind values, payment date, collection method and any evidenced rounding agreement. Produce worker deduction and employer liability separately. Probe general versus construction and a sector exception; wages/bonus on the March–April 2026 boundary; JPY101,000 at 2025 general rate gives JPY555.50 before rounding, hence **JPY555 withheld from wages** under the cited default, versus JPY556 if collected in cash. For ordinary payroll, the [Ishikawa Labour Bureau 2026 notice](https://jsite.mhlw.go.jp/ishikawa-roudoukyoku/content/contents/002623198.pdf) selects the rate by wage closing date: March close paid in April keeps the old rate. Bonus/irregular-payment closing-date treatment and a sealed probe remain open. | No [IN-SYSTEM 2026-09-29] | Source exceptions and required inputs remain open; no JP sealed rule or production probe. |
| JP-EI03 — annual labour-insurance declaration | [MHLW's labour-insurance collection rules](https://www.mhlw.go.jp/stf/seisakunitsuite/bunya/koyou_roudou/roudoukijun/hoken/index.html) require prior-year actual wage totals and premiums to be reconciled with current-year estimated premiums. The 2026 [annual renewal notice](https://jsite.mhlw.go.jp/tochigi-roudoukyoku/newpage_01670.html) gives **1 June–10 July 2026** for filing/payment: settle 2025 at 2025 rates and estimate 2026 at 2026 rates. Workers' compensation is employer-only and has its own industry rate; [MHLW confirms 2026 industry rates are unchanged from 2025](https://www.mhlw.go.jp/stf/seisakunitsuite/bunya/koyou_roudou/roudoukijun/rousai/rousaihoken06/rousai_hokenritsu_kaitei.html), which does not determine a given employer's class.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | Carry the 1 April–31 March wage ledger, industry, insured/noninsured split, prior estimate, annual declaration and payment/correction status. Probe a 2025-to-2026 employment-insurance rate change, bonus and commuting pay, an employee who left midyear, an omitted insured worker and a correction/refund. Obtain the applicable workers' compensation industry/project classifications before computing its amount.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | No [IN-SYSTEM 2026-09-29] | Source exceptions and required inputs remain open; no JP sealed rule or production probe. |

[Yamagata Labour Bureau's 2026 annual-return instructions, p. 8](https://jsite.mhlw.go.jp/yamagata-roudoukyoku/content/contents/002587832.pdf) assign wages to the labour-insurance fiscal year of their **wage closing date**: a March 2026 close belongs in FY2025 even if paid after 1 April. [Ishikawa Labour Bureau's April 2026 notice](https://jsite.mhlw.go.jp/ishikawa-roudoukyoku/content/contents/002623198.pdf) separately instructs applying the new employee/employer EI rate to payroll whose **closing date falls on/after 1 April**. A March close paid in April therefore retains the old rate; do not select by payment date. Store closing and payment dates separately. Required paired probe: the same March close with 31 March versus 1 April payment, then an April close paid in April, checking both deduction and annual-return year. Exact bonus/irregular-payment closing-date treatment still needs its own source disposition.

**Municipal resident-tax provision inventory, 26 September 2026.** The
[Local Tax Act](https://laws.e-gov.go.jp/law/325AC0000000226) supplies the national framework;
the actual municipality's notices and procedures govern the employer's saved amounts and
filings. The linked Osaka and Shinjuku authority pages establish the branches below; their
sample municipalities are not a national rate or form default. These five obligations have no
JP law configuration, input contract, output or saved-input probe. The Act's complete amendment
history and other municipal variations remain to reconcile.

| Obligation                                 | Source-backed branch                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | Required input, output and independent closure case                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | Implemented?              | If not, why?                                                                              |
| ------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| JP-LT01 — prior-year salary report         | [Local Tax Act s.317-6](https://laws.e-gov.go.jp/law/325AC0000000226) and [Osaka's filing guidance](https://www.city.osaka.lg.jp/zaisei/page/0000098160.html) require a salary payment report for current employees, including part-timers and low-paid staff, to the municipality of 1 January residence. Prior-year leavers are reported to the retirement-date municipality, but a leaver whose pay in the exit year was **JPY300,000 or less may be omitted**. Osaka's [2025-income notice](https://www.city.osaka.lg.jp/zaisei/page/0000380781.html) gives **2 February 2026** as that year's filing deadline. This is distinct from the national withholding slip. For an employer that submitted at least **100 national withholding slips** in the second preceding year, Osaka identifies compulsory eLTAX or optical-disk submission. | Preserve every 2025 payment, employee identity, residence/exit date and municipality; generate the municipality's report and filing status. Probe a December 2025 hire, a leaver just below/at/above JPY300,000, a 1 January move, a low-paid current worker and a correction after submission. Probe 99/100 filed slips at the electronic threshold; confirm later-year deadlines rather than hard-coding 2 February. | No [IN-SYSTEM 2026-09-29] | Source exceptions and required inputs remain open; no JP sealed rule or production probe. |
| JP-LT02 — notified monthly resident tax    | [Local Tax Act ss.321-3–321-5](https://laws.e-gov.go.jp/law/325AC0000000226) and [Shinjuku's collection guidance](https://www.city.shinjuku.lg.jp/hoken/file04_04_00001.html) require the designated employer to withhold the **municipality-notified installment** from June through the following May and remit the month's collection by the following month's tenth, adjusted to the next open day where applicable. A changed or late notice changes the schedule; an employer must not recalculate the employee's annual municipal tax from current gross salary or assume an even twelve-way split. [Shinjuku's approved small-employer exception](https://www.city.shinjuku.lg.jp/hoken/file04_00007.html) has distinct December/June remittance deadlines.                                                                             | Capture municipality, notice/assessment year, each effective monthly amount, changed notices, payer designation and any approved remittance exception. Probe December 2025 against the June 2025–May 2026 notice, June 2026 rollover, a changed installment and normal versus approved small-employer remittance. Refuse an amount without the applicable notice or documented successor instruction.                  | No [IN-SYSTEM 2026-09-29] | Source exceptions and required inputs remain open; no JP sealed rule or production probe. |
| JP-LT03 — change of employer or employment | [Local Tax Act s.321-5](https://laws.e-gov.go.jp/law/325AC0000000226) and [Osaka's procedure](https://www.city.osaka.lg.jp/zaisei/page/0000098571.html) require an employee-change report by the **tenth of the month after** retirement, leave or transfer. For a June–December exit, remaining tax may be collected in one amount on the employee's request; for a January–April exit, the remaining amount is collected from salary or retirement pay payable by 31 May where sufficient. A new employer may continue special collection after the transfer filing. The May and insufficient-pay cases need their exact statutory/municipal disposition.                                                                                                                                                                                     | Record exit/transfer date, future payable wages and retirement pay, remaining notice installments, employee election and successor employer. Probe 31 December/1 January and 30 April/1 May, sufficient versus insufficient final pay, requested versus unrequested lump collection and a transfer with a new payer. Produce both the deduction and change report; do not silently zero the balance.                   | No [IN-SYSTEM 2026-09-29] | Source exceptions and required inputs remain open; no JP sealed rule or production probe. |
| JP-LT04 — retirement-income resident tax   | [Local Tax Act s.328](https://laws.e-gov.go.jp/law/325AC0000000226) and [Shinjuku's retirement-tax procedure](https://www.city.shinjuku.lg.jp/hoken/file04_00005.html) treat qualifying retirement pay separately: the payer calculates, withholds, declares and remits local tax to the municipality of the worker's **1 January residence in the payment-due year**, by the tenth of the following month. This is different from settling unpaid monthly notice installments under JP-LT03.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | Capture retirement-payment entitlement date, eligible service/amount, residence and prior retirement benefits; verify statutory deduction, half-taxable exception, local rates and rounding before calculation. Probe a retirement amount and an outstanding notice balance together, a move after 1 January, and a next-month remittance/report. No JP amount is certified.                                           | No [IN-SYSTEM 2026-09-29] | Source exceptions and required inputs remain open; no JP sealed rule or production probe. |
| JP-LT05 — special-collection designation   | [Osaka's salary-report guidance](https://www.city.osaka.lg.jp/zaisei/page/0000098160.html) says employer special collection is the default and lists documented ordinary-collection switches for a prior-year leaver or expected leaver by 31 May, insufficient wage, irregular wage payments, or withholding through another payer. The municipality's required reason code and evidence must accompany the report; employee or employer preference alone is not a switch. Other municipalities' accepted codes and the national statutory scope need reconciliation.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | Preserve the worker's payer, pay cadence, exit date and exemption reason; route the report to ordinary or special collection and capture the municipality's designation. Probe each Osaka reason, a current low-paid worker with enough versus insufficient wage, and an employee with two employers. Do not start or stop monthly deductions on a local label without a notice or valid transfer instruction.         | No [IN-SYSTEM 2026-09-29] | Source exceptions and required inputs remain open; no JP sealed rule or production probe. |

**Independent closure cases:** 2025 annual income at each newly revised basic-deduction
threshold; a 19–22-year-old relative just below/at/above the income eligibility boundary;
December 2025 wage withholding versus 2025 annual adjustment versus January 2026 withholding;
2026 monthly pay in November/December versus 2026 December annual adjustment versus January
2027 withholding and its defense/reconstruction components; a December 2026 wage due in January
2027 versus a December wage due in December, plus the Q&A's exceptional director-bonus and
retroactive-pay dates; resident and nonresident 2026 basic-deduction boundaries at JPY1.32m/3.36m/
4.89m/6.55m/23.5m; annual salary around JPY691k/741k/2.191m/2.193m/2.196m/2.2m under the
2026 salary-income table; a newly eligible dependent with old versus revised declaration on
30 November/1 December 2026; an employee's final 2026 wage before 1 December; qualifying
under-23 dependent with new/old life-policy premiums around JPY30k/60k/120k and the combined
JPY120k cap; 2026 monthly/daily/bonus table selection, 甲/乙/丙 status, and a bonus just below/
above ten times the prior-month wage;
prior-employer slip present/absent; qualifying unpaid 2025 wage and taxable benefit in kind;
JPY1,000 taxable-salary and JPY100 annual-tax rounding edges; last-wage withholding included
versus omitted; 44.99/45/54.99/55 km commuter distances with April–November 2025 history,
and the higher exemption carried through year-end settlement. Minimum-wage cases: Iwate on
30 November/1 December 2025; Yamagata on 22/23 December; Fukushima, Tokushima, Oita and
Kumamoto on 31 December 2025/1 January 2026; Gunma on 28 February/1 March 2026; Akita
on 30/31 March 2026; base
pay just below/at the floor with a bonus, commuting allowance or overtime that cannot cure a
shortfall; and an applicable industry floor above the regional rate. The full 2025/2026 tax tables,
forms, exceptions and non-tax families still need independent verification and persisted probes.
For minimum wages also probe Tokyo on 30 September/1 October 2026 at JPY1,226/1,280;
the Regulation Art. 2 scheduled-hours divisors for day/week/month/piece/mixed pay;
an Art. 15(4) time-limited industry exception; an Art. 7 permit versus mere probation;
an Art. 34 worker report followed by employer action; the separate Art. 35 seafarer route;
and the surviving nationwide JPY5,772/day mining floor against regional floors for workers
with different scheduled daily hours.
For an ordinary Tokyo worker with no higher specific floor or reduction permit, paid JPY220,000
per month with exactly 2,064 annual scheduled hours (172 average monthly hours), Regulation
Art. 2 gives JPY220,000 ÷ 172 =
JPY1,279.069…/hour: it clears the JPY1,226 floor on 30 September but falls below the
JPY1,280 floor on 1 October; JPY220,160 monthly pay would meet the latter at those hours.
This is an independent expected case, not a production result or a rounding-policy finding.
For health/pension, compare a December 2025 acquisition from another employer against the
former salary; a May/June 2026 acquisition around the regular-decision exclusion; eligible
April–June months at 16/17 and covered short-time 10/11 base days; a fixed-pay change versus
variable overtime alone; a two-grade difference versus one grade at a ceiling/floor; and the
third/fourth-month effective boundary. For bonuses, cross JPY1,000 and JPY1.5m pension edges,
split two bonus payments within a month, and cross the JPY5.73m health cap using a previous
employer under the same versus a different insurer, including a March/April fiscal-year reset
and a loss-of-status month. For example, JPY5m earlier standard bonus under the same insurer
and a JPY1m new bonus leave only JPY730k of health-insurance standard-bonus capacity, subject
to the required cumulative declaration/determination; a different insurer starts a separate
JPY5.73m cap. Compare March/April 2026 insurance liability, the May deduction and
the 0.23% child/childcare support amount on a determined grade. These cases need insurer-issued
grade and rate inputs rather than a fresh gross-pay multiplication.

Required inventory includes Labour Standards/minimum-wage law, contracts, hours and premiums,
leave and termination; income/reconstruction-tax withholding, salary/bonus tables, annual
adjustment and special payments; municipal resident-tax instructions; health/care/pension and
employment/work-injury insurance, standard remuneration/bonus grades, insurer/local rates,
enrolment timing and rounding; employer-only obligations; reporting/remittance and corrections.
Use actual prefecture/municipality/insurer, worker status, residence, declarations and histories.
December 2025 annual adjustment must accept relevant earlier-2025 pay/tax facts without assuming
the employee first started work in December. Independent December/January, bonus, joining/leaving,
grade/change, rounding and correction cases remain unexecuted. No JP amount is certified yet.

The 26 September 2026 source inspection found no `seed/jurisdiction/JP/` directory; the
`MY-nihon` lineage has `jurisdiction_code: "MY"` and cannot govern Japan. A complete operative
instrument inventory, lawful sealed versions and focused production/persistence probes remain
required before any JP compliance claim.
